📺 No one has died in this one yet. That you know of.
📺 You are at 74 health.

Participate — or refuse, and let the galaxy watch you say no."
⚠ WARNING: reserves critical. Eat tomorrow or the spiral begins.
⚠ DEHYDRATED: find and treat water today.
No food in the pantry. The village is hungry.
⚠ Haven's pantry is empty. Day 3 of hunger.
Haven couldn't hold. On the third hungry day, people started walking — in different directions. The scattering, again.
🎁 You find a small bundle tucked where you'll find it. No one saw it left there.
"The System flagged your sector. Move. NOW."
(365 kcal of dried meat and clean water. Nothing traceable.)
📦 A package covered in stickers. The card, in loopy handwriting: "Hope you're doing great! Here's some stuff from home!"
Inside: snacks that taste like purple, a tiny flag, and a photo of someone giving a thumbs-up.
Hunger gnaws. The pack is thin — work the green, haul it back, get it named at camp. That's the whole game.
